### Page 2

2Q26 beat: Gigabyte’s 2Q26 revenue was up 41% YoY / 37% QoQ to NT$144bn, or 12% 
/ 9% above our estimate / Bloomberg consensus, which we attribute to the AI server 
business expansion and PC demand pull-in amid memory price hike. GM of 9.6% is lower 
than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, mainly on the rising shipments of 
rack-level AI server and rising cost of key components (e.g. memory, CPU, PCB, etc). 
2Q26 opex ratio is better than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, reflecting the 
improving operational efficiency as shipments ramp up. Overall, 2Q26 net income was 
up 117% YoY / 25% QoQ to NT$6.6bn, or 21% higher than our / Bloomberg consensus.

Earnings revision: We factor in Gigabyte’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 result and raise our 
2026-28E net income by 8% / 14% / 13%, mainly on higher revenues. Our 2026-28E 
revenues are revised up by 16% / 22% / 30%, mainly on more AI servers and higher AI 
server racks’ ASP , considering the migration towards next-generation AI server with 
stronger computing power and more complex design. Our GM is lower given the product 
mix toward AI servers. Our OP income raise is smaller than the GP raise given the R&D 
investments to facilitate AI servers technology development. Our NI raise is smaller than 
the OP income raise given the higher interest expense, reflecting the company’s rising 
debt to support the working capital and capex. The company’s operating cash flow is at 
NT$79bn loss in 1H26 (vs. NT$5bn gain in 2025A).

Valuation: We continue to use near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP . Our 2027E target 
P/E is at 14.7x (vs. previously at 16.8x) applied to diluted EPS, still derived from 
correlation of peers’ forward year P/E to avg. EPS YoY (methodology unchanged), 
reflecting the sector de-rating on AI server ODMs amid the model transition and market

concerns on consumer electronics end demand under the high memory costs. With our 
updated target P/E and earnings estimates, our 12M TP is unchanged at NT$487. The 
target P/E of 14.7x is within the company’s historical avg. and avg.+1std forward trading 
P/E. Maintain Neutral.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is 
based on a 14.7x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Exhibit 4: The target P/E is derived from correlation of

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

M
Foundation
eMemory Technology Inc  | Asia Pacific
An overlooked quantum and IP
play; Initiate at OW
We expect eMemory to benefit as PUF-PQC adoption appears
set to accelerate, driven by NeoFuse adoption, PQC
opportunities, and royalty growth.
eMemory is a leading embedded non-volatile memory (eNVM) and hardware
security IP provider with a royalty-driven business model: The company
generates high-margin recurring royalty revenue through its semiconductor IP

portfolio. We believe eMemory is well positioned to benefit from advanced-node
migration and the emerging post-quantum cryptography (PQC) opportunity (see our
report: Quantum Security – Building Defenses for Q-Day and Beyond), supporting
long-term royalty growth.
Well positioned for advanced-node migration, supporting broader NeoFuse OTP
(one-time programmable memory) adoption: We believe continued migration
toward advanced process technologies is likely to  drive NeoFuse adoption. Higher

wafer values at advanced nodes should further support royalty growth while
creating future NeoPUF (physical unclonable function) opportunities.
Emerging PQC opportunity could create a second long-term royalty engine: As
governments and enterprises transition toward quantum-safe security, hardware-
based key protection is becoming increasingly important. We believe NeoPUF  is well
positioned as a hardware root-of-trust (RoT) solution and could become a

meaningful growth driver alongside OTP.
Initiate at Overweight with a NT$3,330 price target: Our valuation is based on a
residual income model. We forecast a 25% EPS CAGR (2025-28E), driven by royalty
growth, NeoFuse adoption, and security IP.
Key risks to our outlook include: 1) slower-than-expected adoption by key
customers, 2) slower-than-expected PQC adoption, and 3) competition in the PUF-
based PQC solution.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA

eMemory Technology Inc(3529.TWO, 3529 TT)
Greater China Technology Semiconductors | Taiwan

Investment Summary: An overlooked quantum and IP play
eMemory is a leading provider of embedded non-volatile memory (eNVM) and hardware
security IP, with a business model differentiated by its high-margin, recurring royalty
revenue stream. In our view, the market continues to primarily view eMemory as an OTP
IP provider, with limited recognition of the company's potential exposure to emerging
post-quantum security opportunities. We believe the company is well positioned to

benefit from two structural trends: (1) continued migration toward advanced
semiconductor nodes, which should support broader adoption of NeoFuse OTP solutions,
and (2) the emergence of PQC, which could create a new long-term growth opportunity
for NeoPUF. As governments and enterprises accelerate migration toward quantum-safe
security architectures, we believe hardware-based key protection will become increasingly
important. eMemory's NeoFuse-based PUF technology is well positioned to serve as a

hardware root of trust for PQC-enabled systems. While PUF currently contributes only
~1% of the company's royalties, broader adoption could create a second long-term royalty
engine alongside eMemory's traditional OTP business. In addition, we view NeoFuse as
positioned to benefit from advanced-node migration, while potentially creating pull-
through opportunities for future NeoPUF adoption. In our view, given eMemory's IP-
centric business model, rising wafer values and increasing production volumes at advanced

nodes should also support sustained royalty expansion over time.
NeoPUF may offer advantages in advanced-node scaling, low-
power designs, and next-generation hardware root-of-trust
applications...
Among today's hardware root-of-trust makers, Synopsys is currently the most direct
competitor to eMemory. Following its March 2024 acquisition of Intrinsic ID, Synopsys'
security platform is primarily based on SRAM PUF technology, which derives device-

unique cryptographic keys from the start-up behavior of volatile SRAM cells. From a
system design perspective, SRAM PUF solutions require dedicated SRAM resources and
additional stabilization, helper-data, and error correcting code (ECC) circuitry to
reconstruct stable keys across variations in temperature, voltage, and device aging.
Unlike SRAM PUF, which derives entropy from the power-up behavior of memory cells,
NeoPUF generates its fingerprint from quantum tunneling characteristics created by

randomly distributed oxide defects within the gate oxide. These defects are formed
naturally during semiconductor manufacturing and produce an enormous number of
possible device states, enabling each chip to generate a highly unique and statistically
random silicon fingerprint with randomness approaching the theoretical ideal.
More importantly, NeoPUF's near-zero bit-error rate originates from the permanent nature
of the underlying oxide defects and dangling bonds. Once these atomic-scale defects are

formed during fabrication, they become highly stable physical structures. Reconstructing
the original silicon-oxide bonding network would require temperatures exceeding
approximately 700°C, far beyond any normal operating or qualification condition. As a
result, the tunneling characteristics remain essentially unchanged throughout the lifetime
of the device, allowing the same enrolled root key to be regenerated repeatedly with
virtually identical responses years later.

### Page 6

M
Foundation
6
Exhibit 8: NeoPUF vs. SRAM PUF
Source: PUFsecurity
We believe eMemory's moat extends beyond NeoPUF itself. The company has spent years
developing and validating its security IP portfolio, supported by a broad patent base and
expertise accumulated through multiple technology generations. Given the mission-critical
nature of hardware root-of-trust architectures, customers tend to prioritize proven
reliability over adopting new security technologies, resulting in long qualification cycles

and high switching costs.
OTP Business Benefits from Advanced-Node Migration and
Rising Wafer Values
As semiconductor production continues to migrate toward advanced process nodes, OTP
benefits from increasing eMemory's penetration as conventional eFuse solutions face
growing scaling challenges at smaller geometries. At the same time, royalties account for
the majority of eMemory's earnings and are closely tied to customer production. As
manufacturing shifts toward leading-edge nodes, higher wafer values translate into higher

royalty revenue per design win. In addition, customers upgrading their security
architecture at advanced nodes are increasingly evaluating OTP and PUF solutions
together, creating incremental cross-selling opportunities. Consequently, we believe that
advanced-node migration should support both broader OTP deployment and sustained
royalty growth over the long term.

Investment Thesis
Recent correction creates an attractive entry point: We believe the recent share price
correction was triggered by 1Q26 earnings miss and concerns over near-term OTP royalty
growth. However, we view part of the weakness as timing-related, as 1Q26 royalties had
yet to reflect recent foundry price increases that management expects to benefit results
in 2H26. More importantly, investor attention remains almost entirely focused on the core

OTP business, while assigning little value to NeoPUF and eMemory's positioning within
the emerging post-quantum security ecosystem.
The market is overlooking eMemory's long-standing PUF investment: We believe
investors continue to view eMemory primarily as an OTP IP company, overlooking its
multi-year investment in PUF technology. Unlike many companies that only recently
began positioning around post-quantum security, eMemory has been developing NeoPUF

for years and has already secured commercial deployments that contribute revenue today.
Attractive valuation relative to both security and semiconductor IP peers: We believe
eMemory should be assessed through both the quantum security and semiconductor IP
peer groups given its exposure to hardware security and royalty-driven IP licensing.
Relative to quantum security peers, eMemory stands out as one of the few companies
generating meaningful earnings and cash flow. Relative to semiconductor IP peers, its

valuation does not appear demanding despite offering superior profitability, with above
50% ROE, well above most global IP peers. We therefore believe the company's valuation
premium is supported by its earnings quality and business model.
Why we rate the shares OW: We see a differentiated combination of structural royalty
growth, advanced-node migration exposure, and underappreciated post-quantum security
optionality. While the market primarily values eMemory based on its established OTP

franchise, we believe NeoPUF could emerge as a meaningful second growth engine as
hardware root-of-trust adoption expands in the post-quantum era. As a result, we see the
potential for both earnings growth and a gradual re-rating of the stock over time.
Investment Positives
1.    PQC adoption could create a new long-term growth engine for PUF: As
governments and enterprises accelerate the transition toward PQC, hardware-
based key protection is becoming increasingly important. PUF is emerging as a

critical hardware root-of-trust component, enabling secure generation and
protection of large cryptographic keys required by PQC architectures. While PUF
currently contributes only around 1%  revenue for eMemory, we believe adoption
could accelerate meaningfully over the next few years as security requirements
evolve. More importantly, eMemory's IP business model benefits from strong
customer stickiness. As a result, broader PUF adoption has the potential to

become a structural growth driver rather than a one-off licensing opportunity,
creating a second long-term royalty engine alongside OTP.
2. NeoFuse is positioned to benefit from advanced-node migration, while

### Page 9

M
Foundation
Morgan Stanley Research 9
creating additional pull-through opportunities for NeoPUF: As semiconductor
geometries continue to shrink, conventional eFuse solutions face increasing
scaling challenges related to area efficiency, reliability and process compatibility.
NeoFuse offers an alternative OTP architecture that is designed to be more
suitable for advanced nodes, particularly below 28nm, where customers have
fewer commercially available options. We believe this creates a favorable

competitive position for eMemory, allowing the company to expand OTP
penetration as advanced-node adoption increases. In addition, OTP and PUF are
becoming increasingly complementary rather than standalone solutions. As
customers redesign their security architecture around next-generation OTP
solutions, they are more likely to evaluate PUF at the same decision point,
positioning eMemory to benefit from its integrated OTP and PUF portfolio. This
creates a potential cross-selling opportunity, where broader NeoFuse deployment

not only can expand OTP royalties but could also increase future PUF royalties. In
our view, OTP migration may therefore also serve as an important catalyst for
future PUF adoption.
3. Higher wafer values could drive sustained royalty expansion: Unlike many IP
vendors that primarily rely on upfront licensing revenue, eMemory generates
about 70% of its earnings from royalties tied to customer production. As
semiconductor content continues to migrate toward more advanced process

nodes, wafer costs have increased substantially versus mature-node production.
Because royalty payments are generally linked to wafer utilization and production
volume, rising wafer values can translate into higher royalty revenue per design
win. This dynamic creates a favorable business model characteristic: once IP has
been qualified and designed into a product, eMemory can continue to participate
in customers' production growth with limited incremental operating costs.

Therefore,  continued migration toward advanced nodes and higher wafer ASPs
could support long-term royalty growth and operating leverage.
Investment Concerns
1.     Slower-than-expected adoption by key customers: Leading semiconductor
companies tend to be more conservative when evaluating and deploying new
memory and security architectures due to extensive validation and qualification
requirements. Given the increasingly concentrated nature of semiconductor

shipments, delayed adoption by a handful of major customers could slow
eMemory's revenue penetration at foundries and postpone the ramp-up of royalty
revenue.
2. Slower-than-expected PQC adoption:      If enterprise, cloud, automotive, or
government adoption of PQC progresses more slowly than expected, demand for
PUF-based security solutions may take longer to materialize, delaying NeoPUF's
revenue contribution and associated royalty growth.
3. Geopolitical and trade policy risks: Rising geopolitical risks could become an

additional consideration in customers' technology sourcing decisions. This may
affect the pace of design-win conversion and customer adoption, particularly

### Page 10

M
Foundation
10
among global customers with increasingly diversified supply-chain strategies.

eMemory: Earnings  Outlook
Revenue
We expect eMemory's revenue to grow at a 25.9% CAGR over 2025-28E, driven by rising
OTP penetration in advanced semiconductor applications, continued ASP increases across
its IP portfolio, and an expanding royalty base. We also expect growing adoption of
advanced-node OTP solutions to support both licensing and recurring royalty revenue,
while PUF-related products begin contributing meaningfully from 2027 onwards as

hardware security requirements increase across AI, data center, automotive, and industrial
applications. Looking further ahead, we see PUF and post-quantum security-related
opportunities as emerging growth drivers for eMemory. While commercialization remains
at an early stage, increasing demand for hardware root-of-trust architectures and secure
device authentication should gradually expand the addressable market and support a
higher contribution from security-related IP over time.

EPS
We expect eMemory's EPS to increase from NT$25.99 in 2025 to NT$53.31 in 2028,
representing a 27% CAGR over 2025-28E. Earnings growth should be driven by continued
revenue expansion, a growing mix of higher-margin royalty revenue, and the company's
highly scalable licensing model. Given its asset-light business model and minimal cost of
goods sold, we expect much of the incremental revenue to flow through to the bottom
line, supporting sustained profitability and earnings growth.

How our estimates differ from consensus...
Our 2026-28E EPS estimates are below consensus. As eMemory remains relatively under-
covered by the sell-side, consensus estimates are derived from a relatively small analyst
base. While we remain positive on the company's long-term growth prospects, our
forecasts assume a more gradual earnings trajectory over the forecast period, resulting in
earnings estimates below current consensus expectations.

eMemory: Valuation methodology
Our price target is NT$3,330: We derive our price target from a residual income model,
as we believe this methodology is best suited to capturing the long-term value of
eMemory's high-margin IP and royalty business model. We assume an 8.6% cost of equity
(2.0% risk-free rate, 6.0% equity risk premium, and 1.1 beta), a payout ratio of 90%, a
medium-term earnings growth rate of 23.0%, and a terminal growth rate of 4.4%. Based

on these assumptions, our RI model implies an equity value of NT$249bn, or NT$3,330
per share.
Our price target implies 80x 2027e P/E, above the company's historical average of 69x but
below its +1SD valuation band of 97x. We believe this  valuation is warranted by
eMemory's leadership in embedded non-volatile memory and hardware security IP, long-
duration royalty streams, and growing exposure to secular semiconductor security trends,
including post-quantum cryptography and hardware root of trust.

We believe eMemory should be evaluated through both the quantum computing/security
and semiconductor IP peer groups, given its exposure to both hardware security and
semiconductor royalty-driven business models. Relative to quantum and security peers,
eMemory appears attractive on a P/S basis while standing out as one of the few
companies generating meaningful earnings and cash flow. Relative to semiconductor IP
peers, we view Arm as the most relevant benchmark given the similarities in licensing and

royalty business models. Compared with Arm, eMemory's valuation does not appear
particularly demanding despite delivering significantly higher profitability. With a 2027E
return on average equity (ROAE) of 61%, nearly double that of most global IP peers, we
believe eMemory's valuation premium is justified by its superior earnings quality.
Scenario Analysis
Base case: We forecast revenue CAGR of 25.9% over 2025-28E, driven by rising OTP
penetration in advanced semiconductor applications, continued ASP increases across its IP

portfolio, and an expanding royalty base. Under these assumptions, we derive our base-
case valuation of NT$3,330/share.
Bull case: We forecast revenue CAGR of 42% over 2025-28E, supported by successful
NeoPUF adoption among major hyperscale and semiconductor customers. We assume
eMemory expands its penetration into datacenter and AI-related chips, while advanced-
node OTP adoption and royalty growth exceed our base-case expectations. Under this
scenario, we derive our bull-case valuation of NT$4,555/share.

Bear case: We forecast revenue CAGR of 5% over 2025-28E, reflecting slower-than-
expected OTP adoption at advanced nodes. We also assume alternative PUF technologies
gain market traction, limiting NeoPUF penetration and resulting in weaker royalty growth.
Under this scenario, we derive our bear-case valuation of NT$1,865/share.

Company Background
eMemory Technology Inc., founded in 2000 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, is a
leading semiconductor IP provider specializing in eNVM and hardware security
technologies. The company develops and licenses a broad portfolio of memory IP
solutions, including OTP, MTP, flash, PUF, and emerging memory technologies, serving
applications across AI, data centers, networking, automotive, industrial, and IoT markets.

With more than 1,350 patents and technology support extending from mature nodes to
advanced 2nm processes, eMemory has established itself as one of the world's leading
memory IP vendors.
The company's core business is built on embedded memory IP solutions, including OTP,
MTP, and flash technologies, which are widely used for code storage, device configuration,
calibration, and security applications across a broad range of semiconductor devices.
Leveraging its long-standing expertise in embedded memory technologies, eMemory has

gradually expanded its product portfolio to include NeoPUF, NeoFuse, NeoEE, NeoMTP,
NeoFlash, and other next-generation memory and security solutions. These products
target growing demand for advanced semiconductor security, AI infrastructure,
automotive electronics, and future PQC-enabled systems.
The company generated 67.5% of FY25 revenue from recurring royalty income and 32.5%
from license fees, reflecting the scalability of its IP licensing business model.

Key Competitors
Globally, eMemory competes with a range of embedded non-volatile memory (eNVM) and
hardware security IP providers. In the OTP and eNVM IP market, Synopsys (through its

Kilopass acquisition) is generally considered one of the company's closest competitors,
while broader semiconductor IP vendors such as Cadence also participate in selected
overlapping markets. In security-related applications, competition comes from companies
including Rambus, Secure-IC and Intrinsic ID, which offer hardware security, root-of-trust
and PUF-based solutions. In Taiwan, companies such as Faraday and M31 have exposure to

### Page 18

M
Foundation
18
selected memory and security IP markets, although their business focus differs from
eMemory's core offerings. The market remains highly fragmented, with competitive
dynamics varying across process technologies, end-applications and customer
requirements, while foundry-provided memory solutions and internally developed IP at
large semiconductor companies also represent important alternative offerings.
Key Suppliers and Customers
eMemory's customers primarily consist of leading global fabless semiconductor

companies, IDMs and memory manufacturers across consumer, communications, data
center and automotive markets. Revenue is generated through a combination of upfront
licensing fees and recurring royalties. Customers first adopt eMemory's IP during the
design stage and subsequently enter mass production through qualified foundries. Royalty
revenue is then collected based on wafer shipments incorporating eMemory's IP, primarily
from foundries such as TSMC and UMC.

Shareholding Structure
As of April 2026, eMemory's shareholder base was primarily composed of long-term
institutional investors. The largest shareholder was Citibank Custody Singapore
Government Investment Corporation (GIC), with a 6.23% stake, followed by Standard
Chartered Custody for Smallcap World Fund, with 5.45%. Other major shareholders
included Fubon Life Insurance (3.89%), the Labor Pension Fund (3.78%), and board
member Chen Li-Chuan (2.97%). The top ten shareholders were predominantly global

institutional investors and domestic financial institutions, reflecting a relatively diversified
ownership structure with no single shareholder holding a controlling stake.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Eclat (1476.TW)
2Q26 Briefing – Constructive 2H Outlook; Easing Input Costs and 
Healthy New Product Momentum
CITI'S TAKE 
We came away from Eclat’s 2Q26 briefing with a constructive view on the 
2H26 demand outlook, supported by six months of order visibility, healthy 
new product development activity, and new customer wins. Raw material 
headwinds should ease sequentially from 3Q as oil prices normalize and 
price negotiation progress. We slightly trim our earnings estimates 2-3% to 
factor in 2Q26 results. We reiterate Buy with an unchanged TP of NT$460 
(17x NTM P/E). With fundamentals turning more positive h/h, we think at 
11.5x NTM P/E, near the low end of its historical range, downside appears 
limited with a 6% dividend yield providing additional valuation support. 
2H26 outlook remains constructive with 6-month visibility — Mgmt. reiterated 
that order visibility remains 6 months and noted that monthly sales of >NT$3bn is 
achievable. Looking further ahead, mgmt. also maintains a cautiously optimistic 
view on 1H27E, indicating that despite potential uncertainties from geo tension and 
macro conditions, product development requests, customer engagement and long-
term order discussion remain active.
New product momentum stays healthy; wellness contribution at ~15% — New 
product development remains one of the more encouraging messages from the 
briefing. Mgmt. highlighted that: 1) wellness products account for ~15% of sales and 
continue to receive good market feedback, 2) brand customers continue to invest in 
product innovation at a healthy pace, 3) new product development and launch ratios 
continue to trend upward, and 4) Eclat is currently developing products with three 
new brand customers. One has already started placing orders while the other two 
remain under development. 
Margin pressure should ease in 3Q; product mix remains the key swing factor — 
2Q GPM declined q/q mainly due to higher raw material costs and an unfavorable 
mix, as stronger-than-expected carry-over product demand diluted the margin 
benefit from new products. Mgmt. expects raw material pressure to ease in 3Q, 
supported by lower oil prices and ongoing customer price negotiations. The pace of 
margin recovery will still depend on the mix between new and carry-over products. 
Mgmt. will continue to target > 30% GPM in 2H26E. (continued)

Earnings estimate revision and TP — We slightly trim our earnings estimates 2-3%
for 2026-28 mainly to factor in 2Q26 GPM miss. We keep our target price
unchanged at NT$460 based on 2H26-1H27E 17x P/E. We reiterate our Buy on
Eclat as we see fundamentals improving marginally h/h, with input cost pressure
starting to ease and order visibility remaining healthy at 6 months. Meanwhile,
valuation looks increasingly attractive – the stock is trading at 11.5x our NTM EPS,

new the low end of its historical range of 11-34x. With fundamentals turning more
favorable, we see limited downside from current levels. In addition, a 6% dividend
yield provides further downside support while investors wait for earnings
momentum to strengthen.

### Page 5

Eclat (1476.TW)
18 August 2026 Citi Research
5
Eclat
Company description
Founded in 1977, Eclat is a niche apparel ODM/OEM with a unique vertical
integration model. Eclat specializes in high-end functional fabrics and
garments for active wear brands. Its largest brand customers include Nike,
Target, lululemon, and UA. In 2024, Eclat's garment capacity was ~80% in
Vietnam and ~20% in Cambodia. It is currently ramping up new facilities in
Indonesia.

Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Eclat. We like Eclat's strong R&D capability in fabrics
and product innovations, which should give Eclat stronger bargaining power
when negotiating tariffs with customers, supporting a steady sales/earnings
trend vs. other suppliers. We expect ongoing strong order strength from
emerging brands, along with new customer acquisition, to drive sales growth
of 9% y/y in 2026E, while product mix upgrade, positive FX, and rising

efficiency should boost solid margin expansion, driving a record EPS in
2026E. We view current risk/reward as attractive and expect a margin
recovery to serve as a positive catalyst.
Valuation
Our target price for Eclat of NT$460 is based on 17x 2H26H-1H27E EPS, 15%
below its historical average in view of persisting uncertainties on
consumption trend and oil price fluctuations.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 1

M  Research
19 August 2026
Eclat Textile
Diversified customer base supporting growth
Key Points
• New customers and products to support growth into 2H26.
• Margin headwinds should ease vs 2Q26 on lower raw-material cost
pressure.
• Indonesia capacity set to start mass production in 4Q27.
• Favourable 2H comparison base. Eclat maintains broadly stable order
visibility of around six months, although volatility has increased toward
the end of the cycle. New and wellness products have received positive
customer feedback since launch, and their growing contribution should
support gross margins. The company is also working with two to three
new customers, which should lead to shipments over time. We view
positively the company's ability to secure new customers proactively
despite ongoing industry challenges. We expect revenue growth to
accelerate in 2H26, supported by a lower comparison base.
• Raw-material headwinds should ease sequentially. Management
attributed the QoQ gross margin contraction in 2Q to a higher-than-
expected mix of carry-over orders, despite growth in higher-margin
new products, as well as higher raw-material costs. That said, oil prices
have recently stabilised, and the company is negotiating pricing with
brand customers. As a result, we expect gross margin headwinds to ease
relative to 2Q. Overall, we forecast FY26 gross margin of 31.1%, within
management's target range of 28-32%.
• Investing to improve productivity. Eclat expects to direct this year's
and next year's capex towards expanding capacity in Indonesia and
advancing automation across its garment and fabric plants to improve
efficiency. Management expects the new Indonesian capacity to enter
mass production in 4Q27.
Earnings changes: We lift 2026E/27E adjusted net profit by 3.0%/1.3% to
factor in our higher revenue and margin estimates.
Valuation: We cut our target price 12.8% to NT$547.00 (from NT$627.00),
based on an lower multiple of 22x FY26E PER which represents a 6-year
average vs 26 previously (1 standard deviation above average) to factor in
more volatile industry demand
Catalysts: Monthly revenue update, 3Q26 results, management
commentary, outlook from key customers (e.g., Nike, Lululemon, Under
Armour, Gap/Athleta).
Investment Thesis and Recommendation
We expect Eclat's margins to improve further in 2027, driven by product
innovation. Stronger apparel performance should support volume
and drive a potential re-rating. Eclat is well positioned to secure new
customers and orders as consumers preference shifts to smaller brands.
Prior Eclat research.

### Page 2

Mac Eclat Textile
Key Risks to Investment Thesis
• Weaker-than-expected demand recovery, rising labour
costs, order cuts by end customers, and supply-chain
disruptions.
• Geopolitical risks or increasing trade frictions, such as
tariffs.
• Competition among suppliers, leading to lower margins.
Company Description
Eclat is the leading fabric and garment OEM/ODM in Taiwan,
specialising in knitted fabric and garment products in the
production of functional sports/fitness wear. The company

has in-house fabric and garment production. They also sell
part of the produced fabric to third party customers while
keep the remaining in-house for own garment production.
Eclat's client base is well diversified across global leading
brands, including Nike, Under Armour and Lululemon, etc.

Mac Eclat Textile
Earnings estimate revisions
We lift 2026E/27E adjusted net profit by 3.0%/1.3% on our higher sales estimates.
• Revenue: 2026E/27E up 2.0%/1.6% to reflect higher fabric estimate.
• Margins: 2026E/27E gross margins increase by 0.7ppt and 0.4ppt; operating margins
increase 0.4ppt and 0.1ppt respectively, as we expect new product contribution, cost pass
through and other efficiency initiatives to benefit.

Macquarie – Asia and USA

During periods of share price volatility,
recommendations and target prices may
occasionally and temporarily be inconsistent

with the above definitions.
Recommendations – 12 months
12-month target – Expected share price
in 12 months

fair value share price based on the disclosed
valuation methodology
Note: Quant recommendations may differ
from Fundamental Analyst recommendations
This is calculated from the volatility of historical price
movements.
Very high – highest risk – Stock should be expected to
move up or down 60–100% in a year – investors should be

aware this stock is highly speculative.
High – stock should be expected to move up or down at
least 40–60% in a year – investors should be aware this
stock could be speculative.
Medium – stock should be expected to move up or down
at least 25–40% in a year.
Low – stock should be expected to move up or down at
least 15–25% in a year.
* Applicable to select stocks in Asia/Australia/NZ
Note: expected return is reflective of a Medium Volatility
stock and should be assumed to adjust proportionately

with volatility risk
All "Adjusted" data items have had the following
adjustments made:
Added back: goodwill amortisation, provision for
catastrophe reserves, IFRS derivatives & hedging, IFRS
impairments & IFRS interest expense
Excluded: non recurring items, asset revals, property revals,
appraisal value uplift, preference dividends & minority
interests
EPS = adjusted net profit / efpowa*
ROA = adjusted ebit / average total assets
ROA Banks/Insurance = adjusted net profit /average

total assets
ROE = adjusted net profit / average shareholders funds
Gross cashflow = adjusted net profit + depreciation

shares

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: After market hours, Bizlink released its 2Q26 results and held 
an investor call today (21 August). Its gross margin in 2Q26 improved QoQ 
to 30.8% from 28.8% due to ongoing product mix optimisation and rising 
capacity utilisation. We believe Bizlink will be one of the key beneficiaries 
from AI infrastructure (for HPC and semi cap), and rising product 
complexity should underpin its margin expansion in the following years. We 
reaffirm our Buy (1) call. We now forecast an earnings CAGR of 48.2% 
from 45.6% previously over 2026-28E.

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat slightly. Bizlink’s 2Q26 revenue of 
TWD23,281m (+11.6% QoQ, +37.5% YoY) was in line with our estimate 
and the consensus estimate. Its gross margin was 30.7% (vs. 28.8% in 
1Q26 and 31.1% in 2Q25), higher than our estimate (30.2%) but in line with 
the consensus estimate (30.7%). The recovery in gross margin vs. 1Q26 is 
due to product mix improvement and a higher utilisation rate. Its operating 
profit of TWD4,010m (+29.0% QoQ, +39.9% YoY) slightly beat our and the 
consensus estimates by 2.5% and 1.1%, respectively. Thus, its operating 
margin of 17.2% (vs. 14.9% in 1Q26 and 16.9% in 2Q25) was roughly in 
line with our estimate (17.0%) and the consensus (17.3%). Overall, its 
2Q26 net profit of TWD2,982m (+31.1% QoQ, +47.3% YoY) also slightly 
beat our and the Bloomberg estimates by 4.8% and 2.9%, respectively. Its 
2Q26 basic EPS finished at TWD15.28.

Key highlights from results call. During the call, Bizlink mentioned that 
its copper, power and optical solutions should lead it to win more layers of 
increasingly integrated AI infrastructure, and the product complexity (higher 
power density, bandwidth and integration requirement) trend is also positive 
for its gross margin expansion. The acquisition of Interplex Datacom (see 
memo) is expected to close in 2H26 and should strengthen Bizlink’s 
capabilities in precision metal fabrication and mechanical and structural 
components, as well as electrical and connectivity capabilities. We raised 
our 2026-28E EPS by 1-5%, mainly on our more aggressive revenue 
assumptions on its order share win potential.

What we recommend: We reiterate our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD3,065 (from TWD3,053), based on an unchanged PER of 30x 
(equal to a 0.62x PEG over 2026-28E; at the high-end of its past-3-year 
range of 6-34x) applied to our 1-year forward diluted EPS forecast. Key 
downside risks: weaker-than-expected orders from secular growth drivers 
(HPC and semi cap) and slower-than-expected gross-margin expansion.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 1-5% above the consensus, as our 
more aggressive revenue and operating margin assumptions.

21 August 2026

➢ Benefitting from spec upgrade on its copper, power and optic solutions 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising 12M TP to TWD3,065

Company profile 
Founded in 1996 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in 2011, Bizlink Holding (Bizlink) is a vertically-
integrated provider for interconnect solutions for IT & CE (28% of 2025 revenue), electrical appliances 
(16%), automotive (15%) and industrial (39%). Major clients include Dell, HP, Tesla, Polaris, Thermo 
King, Dyson and GE Healthcare.

### Page 5

5

Bizlink Holding (3665 TT): 21 August 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 31 Mar 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and chairperson of Bizlink are not the same individual. Bizlink has 7 directors,
including 3 independent directors, on its board. With backgrounds in engineering (directors) and
materials science/law (independent directors), we view this as positive as we believe that their
wide-ranging experience will help lead Bizlink under the competitive environment.
Capital management 2
Bizlink's payout ratio has gradually declined over the past decade, from 79% in 2016 to 33% in
2025. Considering the company’smergers over this period, we view the drop in payout ratio as
reasonable.
Related party &
transaction 2 No revenue and COGS are generated from related-party transactions – we see no issues.
S Supply chain
management 1
To improve its product quality, Bizlink has high-standard/eco-friendly production processes, and
places high importance on client feedback. The company received an overall positive rating of
93% in its 2023 customer survey. Bizlink has a 100% local sourcing policy (achieved 75% in
2023) – this is to ensure a high standard of services from suppliers and to help reduce its carbon
footprint. Furthermore, Bizlink has more than 500 suppliers globally; only 25% of its procurement
is from the company’stop-10 suppliers. Though we believe Bizlink is relatively safe from material
sourcing risks in the long term, the company has still been impacted by the recent plastic and
copper price hikes.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

M
Update
AllRing Tech Co.  | Asia Pacific
CPO Business Remains Intact;
OW
What's new? AllRing fell 5.5%  vs. Taiex up 0.65% last Friday, mainly on market
concerns about: (1) losing CPO coupling equipment share to MRSI (under Mycronic
AB Group; MYCR.ST; NC); (2) CoW dispensing being disqualified; and (3) heat sink
attachment uncertainty in Rubin Ultra.
Stay OW: Our supply chain checks suggest AllRing's CPO coupling equipment and
next-gen GPU heat sink attachment shares remain intact. CoW dispensing is still

under qualification, with more updates likely in 4Q. We are also confident in
AllRing's engagement with a US advanced packaging supplier, with more details
likely on the August 27 onsite earnings call. The stock trades at 20x 2027e EPS,
which we view as an attractive entry point to participate in the company's long-tail
advanced packaging and CPO opportunities.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AllRing Tech Co. (6187.TWO)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions are:
n Cost of equity (CoE) of 8%, derived from a risk-free rate of 2.0%, beta of 1.0, and a
market risk premium of 6.0%.
n Intermediate growth rate of 16%.
n Cash dividend payout of 65%
n Terminal growth rate of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected CoWoS capacity expansion
n Faster-than-expected adoption of silicon photonics

n Market share gains from peers, led by continuous R&D and China's localization trend
Risks to Downside
n Slowdown in CoWoS capacity expansion
n Allring's "WoS" market share declining
n Slowdown in technology migration such as SoIC and silicon photonics adoption

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Idea
Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
2H26 GM Back to 30%+; 2H26
Momentum Seen Sustained
With the overhang of margin concerns removed, the market
should focus more on the 2H26 outlook. Stay OW.
2Q26 operating profit in line: Off revenue of NT$23,281mn (+12% QoQ/+37% YoY),
GM expanded by 2.0ppt to 30.7% (largely in line with MSe at 29.9% and consensus
at 31.0%),  which management attributed to higher utilization, better efficiency  and a
more favorable revenue mix. Opex of NT$3,144mn (opex ratio of 13.5%) was roughly

in line, leading to operating profit of  NT$4,010mn (OPM 17.2%), 7% higher than MSe
at NT$3,743mn (OPM 16.4%) but in line with consensus at NT$4,012mn (OPM
17.4%). With a non-op loss of NT$113mn, net profit came in at NT$2,982mn (EPS of
NT$15.28), 8% above MSe and 2% above consensus (NT$2,752mn and NT$2,912mn,
respectively).
2H26 growth driven by HPC: Although there is no official guidance on 3Q26, we
estimate revenue could grow 23% QoQ and 55% YoY considering July monthly sales

(NT$9,630mn,+13% MoM/+60% YoY) and normal seasonality in August/September.
We believe HPC should be the major driver, as power interconnects should continue
to see momentum from AI/general servers; meanwhile, data interconnects could
grow along with new ASIC model ramp and GPU sever demand. Besides HPC, its
semi cap revenue should see sustained momentum (+28% QoQ/+52% YoY for 14%
revenue mix in 2Q26). We expect GM to continue expanding on a sequential basis as

scale grows larger and efficiency improves. Management indicated that it will
maintain good capex discipline and is willing to give up potential business
opportunities if the expected return can't  justify the investment. We think this
demonstrates a mindset of prioritizing profitability over pure top-line growth.
Stay OW: Although  GM of 30.7% in 2Q26 was largely in line with the consensus, we
think this should remove an overhang among at least some investors that GM might

continue to hover below 30% for an extended period of time. The focus should now
be directed toward the outlook for 2H26, which we think remains solid. For the
longer term, we believe Bizlink should ride AI capex tailwinds through its offerings in
the power (power whips, busbars, etc.) and data (both copper and optical)
interconnects for volume growth and spec upgrades, as well as the business model
shift in the semi cap equipment segment. We remain OW and maintain our PT at NT

Idea
6
Valuation Methodology
We maintain our price target (base case scenario value) at  NT$3,665, derived from 30x
2027e P/E (unchanged): We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it appropriately captures Bizlink's earnings growth potential. We believe our target
P/E multiple of 30x is justified by an earnings CAGR of 46%   over 2025-28, which implies a
PEG of  0.7. This is similar to the average of 0.7 in 2019-24, when the stock was trading at

an average P/E of 13x for a 19% earnings CAGR.
Bull case scenario value – NT$4,800 (unchanged): We apply a 40x P/E multiple
(unchanged) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario assumes stronger AEC
demand owing to broader adoption within AI servers along with spec upgrades.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,760 (unchanged): We apply a 15x P/E multiple
(unchanged) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario assumes lukewarm data
center demand amid softening IT capex budgets and pricing pressure for low-barrier

products in other business segments.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

1 
2609 陽明 ( YMTC )

報 告 日 期 ：202 6 / 08 / 14 研 究 員 ： 林 奕 頎(Yichi Lin)  
壹、投 資 建 議 
一.公告 2Q / 2026 單 季 稅 後EPS + 1.64 元， YoY + 482%  
2Q / 2026 ，貨櫃 運價指數  單 季 平 均 2,296 ，YoY + 42% 。 
2Q 新 加 坡 海 運燃油  單 季 平 均 每噸 1,133 美元， YoY + 83% 。 
陽 明 公 告2Q / 2026 單 季 營 收 459.23 億元， YoY + 19% ； 單季
營業利益  56.84 億元， YoY + 46%  ； 稅 後 淨 利 57.34 億 元 ，
YoY + 482% ； 2Q / 2026 單 季 稅 後EPS + 1.64 元 ， 優 於 上 一 季
1Q / 2026 的 + 0.41 元，亦 優 於 2Q / 2025 同 期 的 + 0.28 元， 相
較宏遠預估值  + 1.31 元 ， 高 出 25% 。 
二.公告 7 月營收  208.36 億元， YoY + 35% ， 創 最 近22 個 月 新 高 
陽明  1Q / 2026 單季  平 均 每 月 營 收 128.87 億元  。 
陽明  2Q / 2026 單季  平 均 每 月 營 收 153.08 億元  。 
陽明  7 月/ 2026 ， 單 月 營 收 208.36 億元 。 
7 月 營 收 創 最 近 22 個 月 新 高 ， 暗 示 ，3Q 營 運 持 續 轉 強 。 
三.貨櫃運價 SCFI 啟 動 漲 勢 ， 估2026 年稅後 EPS + 8.43 元 
1Q / 2026 單季  SCFI 平均  1,491 ，YoY - 18%。 
2Q / 2026 單季  SCFI 平均  2,296 ，YoY + 42%。 
3Q / 2026 單 季 （ 截 至8 月 13 日） SCFI 平均  3,193，YoY + 112%。 
2026 年 8 月 13 日 SCFI 數值  3,276  （參閱圖 1）。 
SCFI 大漲 ， 等 同 暗 示 陽明  3Q 旺季  營運將 大 幅 跳 升。 
四.美國  企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比 創 54 個 月 新 低 ， 補 貨 力 道 推 升 運 價 
【歷史經驗 】：美國  2020 年 4 月 企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比（參閱圖 2；
含 製 造 、 躉 售 、 零 售 ） ， 攀 升 至 1.74 ，再下 滑 至 2021 年 6 月
1.26 ，補 貨 需 求 激 勵 貨 櫃運 價 上 升，2022 年 1 月 SCFI 貨 櫃 運 價
指數  5,109.6 ，創歷 史 新 高； 陽明  2022 年稅後 EPS  + 51.71 元。 
【目前狀況 】：2026 年 7 月 16 日 發 布 最 新 2026 年 5 月 美 國 企
業 存 貨 銷 售 比 1.28 ，創  54 個 月 新 低 （202 1 年 12 月 ~ 2026 年
5 月 ） ； 凸 顯 美 國 企 業 補貨力 道 將 轉 強； 一 路 下 滑 的 企 業 存
貨 銷 售 比 （參閱 圖 2），成 為 推 升 SCFI 貨 櫃 運 價 指 數的 關 鍵 力
量。隨著 12 月 聖 誕 季 逼 近，美 國 企 業 的 補 貨 力 道 將 持 續 轉 強。 
五.建 議 ：估 EPS 8.43 元， YoY + 72% ；PB 僅 0.5 1 倍 ， 偏 低 ； 買 進

貳、公 司 概 況 
一.公司簡介  
陽 明，全 球 第 9 大 貨 櫃 航 商，以 遠 洋 貨 櫃 航 線（ 歐 洲 線；美 西 線 ；
美 東 線 ）為主 ， 以 近 洋 航 線 （ 亞 洲 線 ） 為 輔。 
2025 年 營 收 比 重 ： 歐 洲 線38%； 美 洲 線 36%； 亞 洲 線 12%； 
其他 14%（ 中 東 、 南 美 、 澳 洲 ） 。

目 前 船 隊 （ 截 至202 6 年 8 月 13 日 ； 依 據Alphaliner 統 計 資 料）： 
總 營 運 艘 數 99 艘，總 營 運 TEU 數為  757,240 TEU，占 全 球 貨 櫃 船
隊 運 力 2.2%： 自 有 ：63 艘 ， 總 TEU 數 382,296  TEU 。 
租 賃 ：36 艘，總 TEU 數 374,944  TEU 。

訂 船 數 量： 待 交 船 數 21 艘 ， 合 計 268 ,360  TEU ， 
約 當 目 前 船 隊運力  35.4%。

二.營運概況及展望  
1. 美國  企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比 創 54 個 月 新 低 ， 運 價 易 漲 難 跌 
美國  企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比 （ 含 製 造 業 、 躉 售 業 、 零 售 業 ） ， 顯 示 相
對 於 銷 售 ， 企 業 存 貨 的 相 對 應 付 能 力 （ 等 同 於 存貨  還 能 應 付
多 久 的 銷 售 需 求 ） ：

企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比 走 高 ， 顯 示 存 貨 過 多 ， 去 化 壓 力 上 升 。 
企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比 走 低 ， 顯 示 存貨 不足 ， 補 貨 需 求 上 升。

受到  新 冠 疫 情 影 響 （2019 年 12 月 新 冠 疫 情 出 現 ，2020 年 3 月
全 球 大 流 行 ） ，美國  2020 年 4 月 企 業 存 貨 銷 售 比（參閱圖 2），
攀升至  1.74 ， 後 續， 一 路 下 滑 至 2021 年 6 月 1.26 ， 帶 來 強 大 的
補 貨 力道 ，美 國 進 口 貨 物 需 求 大 增，海 運 需 求 及 運 價 同 步 上 升 ，
SCFI 貨櫃運價指數在  2022 年 1 月攀升至  5,109.6 的歷史新高水
準 ； 貨 櫃 航 商 陽明 海運  2022 年稅後 EPS 攀升至  + 51. 71 元 。

圖 2：美國企業存貨銷售比（含 製造業、躉售業、零售業）創 54 個月新低； 補貨力道將推升運價

偏重美國市場 的 傳統產業 與 貨櫃航運產業 將受惠
2021年6月 美國 企業存貨銷售比 降至 1.26
激勵2022年1月SCFI貨櫃運價創歷史新高 5,109.6
目前 2026年 5月 最新 美國企業存貨銷售比
明顯走低至 1.28，創最近 54個月新低
有利於推升長期貨櫃運價 。
附註：企業存貨銷售比，包含 製造業、 躉售業 、 零售業 。
數字越低
長期 補貨 需求越強

資 料 來 源 ：財經 M 平方  ； 宏遠

華航 (2610 TT) 
高油價衝擊鈍化

永豐觀點 
油價衝擊耐受度提高，逢低布局等待 4Q 貨運旺季。 
 投資評價與建議 
調升至買進：(1) 油價中長期趨勢向下舒緩成本壓力，(2) 貨運 4Q 仍暢旺，(3) 過
去歷史 PBR 落在 0.5-2.5X，目前為 1.12 倍，調升至買進 ， 目標價23.2 元(1.3X2026 
BVPS)。

ESG 評析 
華航企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。

華航為台灣第一大的國籍航空公司 ：華航成立於 1959 年 ， 為天合聯盟成員 ，2026/6
共有 87 架飛機，客機 69 架(+0 架)，貨機 18 架(+0 架)，2026 年底預計 92 架，
2026 年 1-7 月客運運量較去年同期+4.5%，客運運能較去年同期-1.8%，平均載
客率 78.8%，較去年同期增加 5.1 個百分比，搭載 711 萬人(+4.7%YoY)，貨運量
成長 6%，貨運 yield 成長 19.4％，載貨率 69%，較去年同期提升 2.5 個百分比，
營收結構為客運 58%、貨運 33%、其他 9%。1H26 年客運航線別東北亞 30%、
北美佔 21%、東南亞佔 18%、中國佔 8%、歐洲 13%、香港佔 2%、大洋洲佔 7%，
貨運以北美最高佔 61%， 歐洲佔13%、 東南亞佔15%、 中國佔4%、 東北亞佔3%、
香港佔 3%，大洋洲佔 1%。 
2Q26 稅後獲利 14.1 億元(-63%YoY)：華航 2Q 客運營收 346 億元(+12%YoY)，
貨運營收 237 億元(+44%YoY)，整體營收 638 億元(+24%YoY)，即使航空燃油來
高漲，但受惠客貨運 yield 提升致 2Q 稅後獲利 14.1 億元(-63%YoY)， 稅後EPS 為
0.23 元。7 月營收 202.6 億元(+25%YoY)，8 月熱門航點載客率保持 85%以上，
9 月中秋連假挹注，預估 3Q 營收 642 億元(+26%YoY)，稅後獲利 48.1 億元
(+63%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 0.78 元。

美元/桶(+66%YoY)， 用油桶數813.1 萬桶(+2.9%YoY)， 1H26 航空燃油成本 374
億元 (+56%YoY)，佔成本 38%。2Q26 新加坡 航空 燃油均價159 美元 / 桶
(+63%YoY)，7-8 平均油價 141 美元/桶(+63%YoY)，研究部假設 3Q 和 4Q 航空
燃油為 135 美元/桶(+56%YoY)和 115 美元/桶(+31%YoY)， 預估2026 年為 123.7
美元/桶(+55%YoY)。 
機隊進度：2026/6 為 69 架客機，預計年底新增 5 架至 74 架，其中 A321neo 新
增 5 架，A350-900 新增 1 架，B787-9 新增 2 架，退租或淘汰 A330-300 1 架和
737-800 2 架，除了增加區域航線運能外，並增加歐洲和大洋洲航班。貨機部份目
前 8 架 744F 和 10 架 777F 共 18 架，今年未新增機隊，未來增加777 系列 12 架
並逐步淘汰 744F，保持國籍貨機規模最大的優勢，受惠 AI、半導體和伺服器的相
關產品出口暢旺，帶動台灣貨運市場相對其他區域成長快速。

(+11%YoY)， 貨運營收853 億元(+28%YoY)， 稅後獲利159.5 億元 （+8.5％YoY），
稅後 EPS 為 2.60 元，每股淨值 17.8 元。

                                        2026 年 8 月 17 日

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

神基 (3005 TT)  Buy

營收低谷已過，且評價偏低，給予 Buy 的投資建議  
投資建議 ： 考量公司營收低谷已過 ，2H26 營收表現將優於 1H26，
且評價偏低，給予 Buy 的投資建議。  
2Q26 營收 YOY +17.37% ，成長主要來自於電子件和航太扣件：
2Q26 神基營收為 115.13 億元， QOQ +23.68% ， YOY +17.37% ，
營收成長主要來自於電子件和航太扣件 ， 營收 YOY 分別為 +27%
和 +40% ，其中電子件裡面，以強固型電腦表現較為強勁，漢通
則有部分受到筆電需求下滑的影響 。 機構件營收則為 YOY -1% ，
主要是受到筆電需求下滑的影響，不過營收 QOQ 為 +9%，顯示
需求有回溫跡象。毛利率較 1Q26 增加，雖有受到原物料漲價而
影響毛利率，不過因為較高毛利的強固型電腦拉貨強勁，使得
整體毛利率逆勢成長。  
2H26 營收將呈現旺季水準，其中強固型電腦產品為主要成長動
能： 公司給予營收展望為 1H26:2H26=48~50:50~52 ， 2026 年營
收 YOY 為 5~10% ，並表示有望達高標或是優於展望。主要成長
動能為強固型電腦產品，由全球軍事預算提升所帶動，除了軍
事外，公用事業和大眾運輸也有專案在進行，故公司認為 2026
年強固型電腦營收 YOY 為 20~30% ， 有機會高於30%。漢 通2026
年營收 YOY 為 10~16% ， 主要是強固型電腦的強勁成長所帶動 ，
不過受到筆電需求下滑的影響，整體成長有受到拖累。綜合機
構件方面， 2026 年營收 YOY 為 -8~-12% ，主要是受到筆電需求
下滑的影響，不過有看到 2H26 筆電需求有較 1H26 回溫。汽車
機構件 2026 年營收為 YOY 接近 10% 的成長。毛利率方面，雖
然有受到記憶體漲價的影響，不過主要成長動能的強固型電腦
產品毛利率高於整體平均，一正一反下，預估 2026 年毛利率與
2025 年相當。綜合以上，預估 2026 年營收為 436.09 億元， YOY 
+10.55% ；稅後純益為 54.41 億元， YOY +4.10% ；稅後 EPS 8.72
元。預估 2027 年營收為 473.87 億元， YOY +8.66% ；稅後純益
為 60.50 億元， YOY +11.18% ；稅後 EPS 9.69 元。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG(Environment, Social, Governance) ：

公司簡介：  
神基 (3005 TT) 成立於 1989 年，為聯華神通集團旗下的神達電腦 (3706 TT) 與
美國奇異航太部門（ GE Aerospace ）共同合資成立，為全球第二大國際攜帶
型強固型電腦品牌商，產品應用領域涵蓋軍方、警方、公共事業、通訊、製
造、以及交通運輸等。旗下的漢通科技為國際專業光機電解決方案的供應
商，主要產品包含電源暨新能源解決方案、工業顯示器解決方案、模具原型
打樣解決方案等等。機構件的部分主要分為兩大領域：綜合機構件（筆電與
遊戲機）與汽車機構件，神基擁有領先的 MHCM 技術（ Multi-medium Heat 
& Cool Molding ，多介質急冷急熱成型技術）主要用於強固型電腦及電子機
構件；汽車機構件的部分，則主要生產輕金屬壓鑄製程的汽車內構件，其中
安全帶轉軸為全球市佔率第一。神基另有投資豐達科 (3004 TT) ，持股
36.77% ，有編入公司的合併報表，為航太發動機扣件的製造商。營收比重方
面， 2Q26 電子件佔 54% ，機構件佔 34% ，航太扣件佔 12% 。

2Q26 營收 YOY +17.37% ，成長主要來自於電子件和航太扣件：  
2Q26 神基營收為 115.13 億元， QOQ +23.68% ， YOY +17.37% ；毛利率為
31.69% ，較 1Q26 增加 0.63 個百分點 ； 稅後純益為15.73 億元 ，QOQ +44.65% ，
YOY +12.19% ；稅後 EPS 2.52 元。營收成長主要來自於電子件和航太扣件，
營收 YOY 分別為 +27% 和 +40% ，其中電子件裡面，以強固型電腦表現較為
強勁，漢通則有部分受到筆電需求下滑的影響。機構件營收則為 YOY -1% ，
主要是受到筆電需求下滑的影響，不過營收 QOQ 為 +9% ，顯示需求有回溫
跡象。毛利率較 1Q26 增加，雖有受到原物料漲價而影響毛利率，不過因為
較高毛利的強固型電腦拉貨強勁，使得整體毛利率逆勢成長。  
2H26 營收將呈現旺季水準，其中強固型電腦產品為主要成長動能：  
公司給予營收展望為 1H26:2H26=48~50:50~52 ，2026 年營收 YOY 為 5~10%，

3 群益投顧 
並表示有望達高標或是優於展望。主要成長動能為強固型電腦產品，由全球
軍事預算提升所帶動，除了軍事外，公用事業和大眾運輸也有專案在進行，
故公司認為 2026 年強固型電腦營收 YOY 為 20~30% ，有機會高於 30%。漢
通 2026 年營收 YOY 為 10~16% ，主要是強固型電腦的強勁成長所帶動，不
過受到筆電需求下滑的影響，整體成長有受到拖累。綜合機構件方面， 2026
年營收 YOY 為 -8~-12% ， 主要是受到筆電需求下滑的影響 ， 不過有看到 2H26
筆電需求有較 1H26 回溫。汽車機構件 2026 年營收為 YOY 接近 10% 的成
長。  
毛利率方面，雖然有受到記憶體漲價的影響，不過主要成長動能的強固型電
腦產品毛利率高於整體平均，一正一反下，預估 2026 年毛利率與 2025 年相
當。  
無人機佈局方面， 2026 年無人機佔強固型電腦約 5~10% ，公司希望 2027 年
能佔 8~12% ，主要是 2026 年無人機的案子較 2025 年多 10 倍，歐美客戶都
有來接洽，未來會陸續發酵，加上台灣近期立法院剛通過的 634 億元的無人
機預算，包括為期 6 年共 442 億元的無人載具產業發展統籌型計畫，以及
192 億元的各部會無人機採購預算，故看好未來幾年神基在無人機解決方案
的成長性。  
綜合以上，預估 2026 年營收為 436.09 億元， YOY +10.55% ；稅後純益為
54.41 億元， YOY +4.10% ；稅後 EPS 8.72 元。預估 2027 年營收為 473.87 億
元， YOY +8.66% ；稅後純益為 60.50 億元， YOY +11.18% ；稅後 EPS 9.69
元。  
投資建議：  
考量公司營收低谷已過， 2H26 營收表現將優於 1H26 ，且評價偏低，給予
Buy 的投資建議。

  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

3481.TT 群創 
 2H26 消費性面板承壓 惟轉型效益逐漸顯現

[法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
群創光電主要從事 TFT-LCD 面板 、LCD 模組及相關顯示器產品的研發 、 設計 、 製造與
銷售。產品應用涵蓋 TV、NB、手機、車用及商用顯示等领域。近年積極轉型，深化
Non-Commodity 和 Non-display 業務，擺脫面板報價對於公司營運的影響。   
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
區間操作 55 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，70 
【大盤指數/股價】45309 / 49.5

➢ 2Q26 消費性面板提前拉貨，惟車用產品出貨降溫 
2Q26 營收約 637 億元，QoQ-4.42%，YoY+13.28%，主要受非顯示器
業務下滑逾 10%所拖累，觀察中國車市 1H26 內需疲弱，加上美國政府
取消電動車稅收抵免補助，導致車用業務營收貢獻不如預期 ；反觀消費
性面板 ， 受惠於世足賽事期間 ， 帶動TV 面板拉貨動能 ， 再加上記憶體等
零組件漲價因素，促使 NB 品牌客戶提前備貨，故消費性面板出貨優於
先 前 預 期 。 2Q26 毛 利 率 為 14.56% ， 稅 後 淨 利 45.32 億 元 ，
QoQ+178.1%， 其中近30 億元來自於認列虧損扣抵之遞延所得稅資產 ，
EPS 為 0.57 元。  
➢ 3Q26 NB 面板拉貨動能轉弱，拖累營收表現 
在消費性面板營收貢獻下滑影響之下 ， 預估3Q26 營收大約為 603.68 億
元，QoQ-5.23%，YoY+4.41%。考量 3Q26 TV 面板報價於 7 月、8 月
呈現緩步下降跡象 ， 恐使利差收窄 ， 預估3Q26 毛利率約 14.38，EPS 為
0.75 元，QoQ+32.95%，YoY+8,907%。 
➢ 2026 年預期仍以車用業務作為營收主要成長動能 
展望 2026 年，考量記憶體漲價的影響短期不易消除，面板本業營收恐
承壓，預期群創的營收成長動能仍將來自非顯示器領域，其中，車用方
面，雖然前述表示出貨下滑，然而 2026 年仍受惠於 Pioneer 併表，帶
來顯著營收貢獻。而關於半導體布局，預期 FOPLP 2H26 可望實現月產
能 6,000 萬顆，穩定貢獻營收，惟其占比仍相對較小。整體而言，預期
2026 年營收約 2,538.9 億元， YoY+11.98%，毛利率約 14.49%，
YoY+6.26ppts，稅後淨利 180.46 億元，YoY+7,125%，主要受惠於售
廠所認列之處分利益，EPS 為 2.26 元。 
➢ 公司積極轉型，惟短期貢獻仍小，建議區間操作，目標價 55 元 
看好公司積極轉型，未來在 Non-Display 業務營收占比大幅提升後，改
善過往傳統顯示器景氣循環的影響 。 評價方面，目前股價淨值比為 1.8X，
位處歷史區間上緣，主要受到 FOPLP 以及後續 TGV 題材的帶動，惟考
量 FOPLP 業務營收占比仍相對較小 ， 加上TGV 順利量產時程落在 2028
年，短期未有實質貢獻，並且考量 2026 下半年記憶體漲價恐使顯示器
業務承壓，故維持區間操作建議，目標價 55 元(2026F PB 2X)。

3481 群創 加權指數

個股報告

公司簡介 
群創光電股份有限公司成立於 2003 年，為面板製造商，主要從事 TFT-LCD 面板、
LCD 模組及相關顯示器產品的研發、設計、製造與銷售。產品應用涵蓋電視、筆記型
電腦、手機、車用及商用顯示等领域。近年聚焦雙軌轉型策略，深化非商品化 (Non-
Commodity)和非顯示器(Non-display)業務 ， 以提升毛利率並擺脫面板報價對於公司
營運的影響 。2026 年營收占比預估分別為 Non-display43%、Non-Commodity 13%
以及 Commodity 44%。 
圖 1：營收產品組合

資料來源：國票投顧 
2Q26 消費性面板提前拉貨，惟車用產品出貨降溫 
2Q26 營收約 637 億元，QoQ-4.42%，YoY+13.28%，營收較 1Q26 衰退且不如預
期，主要受到非顯示器業務下滑逾 10%所拖累，觀察中國車市 1H26 內需疲弱，加上
美國政府取消電動車稅收抵免補助，導致車用業務營收貢獻不如預期；商用面板表現
亦不如先前預期 ， 推測應與多數企業將有限預算聚焦於AI 相關基礎設施 ， 導致傳統商
用硬體更新週期早到延長，進而影響商用面板拉貨動能；反觀消費性面板，受惠於世
足賽事期間，帶動 TV 面板拉貨動能，再加上記憶體等零組件漲價因素，促使 NB 品
牌客戶提前備貨，故消費性面板出貨優於先前預期，為營收下檔提供支撐。獲利表現
方面，雖然 2Q26 消費性面板營收占比增加，然而由於 2Q26 TV 面板報價上漲，仍
推升該事業群毛利率，2Q26 毛利率為 14.56%，QoQ+0.13ppts，YoY+6.18ppts，
稅後淨利 45.32 億元，QoQ+178.1%，其中近 30 億元來自於認列虧損扣抵之遞延所
得稅資產，EPS 為 0.57 元。 
3Q26 NB 面板拉貨動能轉弱，拖累營收表現 
展望 3Q26，考量 NB 品牌客戶已於 2Q26 提前拉貨，且預期在記憶體等零組件漲價
情況下，消費者購買意願降低，將導致品牌廠面臨較長的庫存調整期間，恐使 3Q26
乃至 2H26 面板拉貨動能轉趨疲弱。而 TV 面板方面，由於群創聚焦於 50 吋以下的
產品 ，相較於同業積極朝大尺寸面板生產 ， 其客戶黏著度相對較高 ，MNT 則持續受惠
於高階電競及 OLED 機種的出貨帶動，故預期於 TV、MNT 面板出貨仍可維持微幅成
長，預期整體消費性面板 3Q26 營收預計下滑近 10%。商用面板方面，預期仍有一定
換機需求，故預計 3Q26 營收貢獻大約與 2Q26 持平。非顯示器領域方面，預期車用

市場短期復甦動能相對緩慢，而 FOPLP 業務營收貢獻仍相對較小，故預期 3Q26 該
部門業務營收貢獻與 2Q26 大致持平。整體而言，在消費性面板營收貢獻下滑影響之
下，預估 3Q26 營收大約為 603.68 億元，QoQ-5.23%，YoY+4.41%。獲利表現方
面，考量3Q26 TV 面板報價於 7 月、8 月呈現緩步下降跡象，加上出貨動能疲弱導致
稼動率下滑，恐使利差收窄，故即便產品組合優化，仍預估毛利率恐微幅下滑，預估
3Q26 毛利率約 14.38%，QoQ-0.18ppts，YoY+6.5ppts，稅後淨利 60.25 億元，
QoQ+32.95%，YoY+8,907%，EPS 為 0.75 元，投研部此處以 1H26 所公告售出三
座舊世代廠房合計近 200 億元，於 2H26 至 1H27 分階段認列處分利益進行預估。 
2026 年預期仍以車用業務作為營收主要成長動能 
展望 2026 年，考量記憶體漲價的影響短期不易消除，面板本業營收恐承壓， 預期群
創的營收成長動能仍將來自非顯示器領域 ， 其中 ， 車用方面 ，雖然前述表示出貨下滑 ，
然而 2026 年仍受惠於 Pioneer 併表，帶來顯著營收貢獻。而關於半導體布局，預期
FOPLP 2H26 可望實現月產能 6,000 萬顆，穩定貢獻營收，惟其占比仍相對較小。整
體而言，預期 2026 年營收約 2,538.9 億元，YoY+11.98%，毛利率約 14.49%，
YoY+6.26ppts，稅後淨利 180.46 億元，YoY+7,125%，主要受惠於售廠所認列之處
分利益，EPS 為 2.26 元。2027 年預估面板業務仍將受到記憶體漲價影響，導致消費
性面板拉貨動能承壓，而考量更高階的半導體封裝布局 如 TGV 短期仍難以貢獻實質
營收，故預估 2027 年營收成長動能相對有限，預估 2027 年營收 265 億元，
YoY+4.26%， 毛利率14.57%， 稅後淨利101.58 億元 ，YoY-43.7%，EPS 為 1.27 元。 
公司積極轉型，惟短期貢獻仍小，建議區間操作，目標價 55 元 
整體而言，群創透過出售廠房，改善折舊所帶來的獲利壓力，並且預期後續仍將有售
廠計畫 ， 有助提升獲利表現 。 在轉型方面 ， 公司積極發展FOPLP， 且已陸續貢獻營收 ，
同時公司目前亦有布局光通訊產業的計畫，看好未來在 Non-Display 業務營收占比
大幅提升後，改善過往受到傳統顯示器景氣循環的負面影響。評價方面，過往股價淨
值比區間約為 0.4X~2.4X，目前股價淨值比為 1.8X，位處歷史區間上緣，主要受到
FOPLP、光通訊以及後續 TGV 題材的帶動 ， 惟考量FOPLP 業務營收占比仍相對較小 ，
加上 TGV 順利量產時程落在 2028 年，短期未有實質貢獻，並且考量 2026 下半年記
憶體漲價恐使顯示器業務承壓，故維持區間操作建議，目標價 55 元(2026F PB 2X)。

3533 嘉澤端子(LOTES)

報告日期： 2026/08/18 研 究 員： 洪瑞陽  (Ryan Hung ) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 營收 95.69 億元 YoY +14.79% ，EPS17.68 元 
2Q26 合併營收  95.69 億元， QoQ +  2.59% 、YoY +  14.79% ，續創
單季同期新高；惟三率全面走弱：毛利率  45.47% (季減  4.08 個百
分點、年減  5.53 個百分點 )、營益率  21.97% (季減 7.07 個百分
點、年減  9.20 個百分點 )、稅後純益率  20.74% (季減  4.95 個百
分點 )。單季稅後純益  19.84 億元、EPS 17.68 元、季減  17.27% 。
公司公告  7 月營收  33.69 億元、月增  8.88% 、YoY +  18.60% ，
持續走高。 1~7 月累計  222.80 億元、 YoY +  17.44% ，單季毛利
率轉弱，主因原料成本高檔、加上記憶體缺料使新平台放量遞延，
營運短期承壓。

二、2Q26 為營運谷底，主因原物料上漲與產品遞延  
2Q26 毛利率季減  4.08 個百分點至  45.47% ，主因並非產品組合
(高毛利伺服器占比反由  51.02% 升至  55.36% )，而是原料成本承
壓：銅、金、塑膠三大原材料 大漲，去年低價庫存於  1Q 用罄後、
2Q 起 反 映市 場 高 價 ，油 價 則須 回 穩才能 議 得 較 佳 塑膠 與 運 費報
價。加以  CPU、記憶體、 PCB、主被動元件全面缺料 (以記憶體最
嚴重 )，伺服器  ODM 平均板量由  2Q26 約 1.49KK/ 月降至  7~8 
月約  1.32KK/ 月，Intel /AMD 新平台放量遞延約兩季 ； 且 2Q26 幾
無新品可轉嫁成本，原料漲價 侵蝕 毛利率。終端庫存偏低、需求
無虞，客戶端降規趨勢對板量提升反有幫助， 推估 2Q26 為本波
營運谷底。

三、新平台放量與  ASP 升級趨勢不變，後市可期  
明年 放量趨勢不變 ，僅為遞延。公司表示， Intel Birch Stream 現
占 Intel 40% 、2027 年可升至 50~70%，Oak Stream 2027 年 Q1–
Q2 放量 (占 20~30% )；AMD Venice 現滲透率不到  3%、預估 4Q26
小量、2027 年突破 30%。CPU Socket 針腳數由目前平均  6,000 餘
pin 朝 7,000 ~8,000 pin 邁進 (開發中已有 1 萬 pin )， 帶動socket ASP 
明 顯 提 升 ， 即 使 短 期 板 量 受 缺 料 壓 抑、 仍 推 升 伺 服 器 營 收
(1Q26 /2Q26 伺服器  YoY +65% /+70% ) 。高 ASP 新品方面：
SOCAMM 2026 年 占 比 已 逾1% 、 優 於 預 期 、 已 導 入 NVIDIA 
Vera ；QD 7 月量產、下半年有機會占 2~3%；NPO Socket 待客戶
放量此類新品毛利率雖低於區間、對營收與  EPS 貢獻大。公司維
持 AI 營收占比  20% 之中期目標、預計 2~3 年內達成 。

四、預估 2026 /2027 年稅後  EPS 為 82.08 /116.18 元 
由下而上、分應用別推估， 2026 年營收 396.3 億元、YoY +  17.3%，
毛利率  46.90% ，稅後純益  92.4 億元、 YoY +  17.4% ，EPS82.08 
元； 2027 年營收  500.9 億元、 YoY + 26.4% ，毛利率回升至  
48.10% ，稅後純益  130.8 億元、 YoY +  41.6% ，EPS 116.18 元、
YoY + 約 42% 。成長動能來自伺服器新平台放量與針腳數升級帶
動之  ASP 提 升 ， 伺 服 器 營 收 2026 /2027 年 分 別YoY + 約 
68% /45% 、占比由  2025 年 40% 拉升至  57% /65% 。

五、投資建議：買進，目標價  2,320 元 
目前股價因 2Q26 毛利率低於預期， 原料與缺料壓力屬 短期，新平
台放量與  ASP 升級趨 勢 不變，嘉澤 產品與所屬 產業仍在上 行趨
勢，隨著新平台與新產品放量，後市可期， 2027 年獲利可望大幅
回升 ，目前 股價已反映至相對低點，因此給予合理 AI 成長股本益
比 20 倍，2027EPS 116 元，目標價 2320 元，建議買進逢低布局。

貳、公 司 概 況 
一、公司簡介  
嘉澤端子於 1986 年成立於臺灣基隆市，專精於設計與製造連接器
及機構件。公司產品包含各種連接器及其零組件，依照種類可分
為 CPU Socket 、記憶體 插槽、 I/O 連接器、 PIC 連接器等。其中
CPU Socket 進入障礙最高， 嘉澤 在全球擁有超過 4,000 件連接器 /
連接線專利 。專業設計及製造連接器 /連接線給伺服器、個人電腦、
汽車、工控及醫療 。依 Bishop 統計，在電腦暨週邊連接器廠商中
全球排名第四，不分行業別排名全球排名第十八。 嘉澤 為全球前
三大中央處理器插座供應商 ，高度製程整合，包括產品設計、模
治具及自動化設備開發、射出成型、金屬沖壓、電鍍、組裝等，
能更有彈性的服務客戶 。總公司與研發中心位於台灣基隆與台北
南港，而主要的生產製造據點則分佈於中國大陸 (廣州、蘇 州、湖
南等地 )以及越南 (太平廠 )

二、原物成本上升、新產品遞延 放量 造成短期營運低潮  
2Q26 毛利率季減  4.08 個百分點至  45.47% ，明顯低於過去兩年
51% 之 水 準 ， 主 因 並 非 產 品 組 合( 高 毛 利 伺 服 器 占 比 反 而 由 
51.02% 升至  55.36% )，而是 兩大 原料成本 承受巨大壓力， 三大主
要 原 材 料 為 銅 、 金 、 塑 膠 ， 其 占 營 業 成 本 比 重 已 由 去 年 約 45 –
50% (各約  15% )攀升至目前約  65%。去年的 低價庫存於  1Q 用罄
後，2Q 起成本反映市場 原物料 高價，油價則牽動塑膠價格與運輸
成本 ，須待油 價回穩才 能議得較佳價 格。另一 方面，CPU 、記 憶
體、 PCB 、主 被動 元件全 面缺料 ，其中 以記 憶體 最嚴重 ，客戶 端

三、 預估 現為營運谷底， 未來新產品放量 趨勢不變  
因原物料與產品遞延問題，預估現為營運谷底，油價雖還在高檔
但也回穩，且新平台放量趨勢是必然，只是遞延，放量可期。  
Intel 平台 ：Eagle Stream 滲透率接近  50% ；新世代  Birch Stream 
快速放量，目前占  Intel 整體比重已達  40% ，公司預期  2027 年
順利可升至 50~70% ；再新一代  Oak Stream 目前仍在驗證中，預
計 2027 年 Q1~Q2 放量， 滲透率 有機會 20~30% 。 
AMD 平台： 現行主力為  SP5 (Turin )；新世代  Venice 目前滲透率
不到  3%，2026 年第四季小量、 2027 年才大量 ，公司依目前 預
期 2027 年  Venice 應突破  AMD 之 30% 。AMD 進度較  Intel 
為慢。以整體  server CPU 供給概估， Intel /AMD 約各半。  
架構趨勢 (ARM vs x86 )：公司表示即使  ARM 比例升至 40 ~45% ，
x86 仍約占 55~60% ，且  x86 之 CPU 量體相對現況仍有翻倍空
間；針對 ARM base ，已與一家  CSP 合作開發  ARM base socket ，
預留下一波架構轉換之切入點。

CPU Socket 針 腳 數 持 續 增 加 ， 帶 動 單 價 結 構 性 上 行 ： 目 前 平 均 
6,000 餘 pin ，明年  Venice 等新品朝  7,000 ~8,000 pin 邁進，開
發中已有  1 萬 pin 產品。公司強調產品日益複雜 ，不能單純以  
pin 數衡量，但單價可往上走、且仍有議價空間，整體  ASP 有明
顯提升 趨勢 。此為伺服器營收成長的關鍵 ，即使短期出貨板量受
缺料壓抑， ASP 升 級 仍 推 升 伺 服 器 營 收(2026 Q1 伺服器  YoY 
+65% 、Q2 +70% )。 
圖八、 Oak 、Venice 等新產品於 4Q26 後開始放量

圖九、產品演進， CPU S ocket 針腳數持續增加

資料來源：公司法說會簡報  
另外，公司有許多高 ASP 新產品，毛利率低於區間但對於營收和
EPS 貢獻大。 SOCAMM (AI 伺服器用壓接式記憶體模組 )：2026 年
營 收占 比 已 超 過 1% 、 優 於 預期 ， 稼 動 率達 90% 以 上； 已 導 入
NVIDIA Vera 平 台 。 伺 服 器 改 用 SOCAMM 之 原 因 為 水 冷 導 入
後、直立式  DDR 難以設計水冷流道，故改採壓接式模組 (CAMM 
最初用於  NB)。QD(Quick Disconnect ，液冷快接頭 )：7 月已量產、
快速擴張中， 7 月占比約  1%；切入一家  CSP 。公司表示若進度
順利，以 7 月占比推估，下半年有機會占 2~3%。其毛利率低於
公司平均，惟  ASP 很高、營收貢獻強。 NPO Socket (近封裝光學 )：
已開發完成、量產準備就緒，等客戶放量 ，與嘉基分工。 總結來
說，隨著伺服器新平台以及新產品加入，中長期營收持續走高，
雖毛利率暫時承壓，但營收放大，獲利仍成長， 公司維持  AI 營
收占比  20% 之中期目標，預計 2~3 年內達成；SOCAMM、QD 等
產品持續放量、一切照原訂規劃進行。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估 
一、預估  2026 年稅後 EPS 為 82.08 元， YoY +17.4%  
以公司  2Q26 營收為基礎、採由下而上分應用別逐季推估，考量
伺 服 器 新 平 台 放 量 與 針 腳 數 升 級 帶 動 之 ASP 提升、
SOCAMM /QD 放 量 ，並 反映 桌 機/筆 電之 季 節性 與平 台 交替 ， 預
估  3Q26 /4Q26 營 收 分 別 為 100.41 /106.93 億 元 ， 全 年 營 收 
396.30 億元、YoY +17.3% 。獲利部分，毛利率由  2Q26 之 45.47%

2026/08/14 投資速報

電子–電子零組件 
嘉澤 (3533.TT) 
原物料成本壓力未解，獲利修復待 2027

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com

嘉澤 2Q26 稅後 EPS 為 17.68 元，低於研究部原估 21.28 元。
受到金、銅、塑膠等原物料價格大幅上漲影響，且嘉澤為維持市佔
自行吸收成本，加上記憶體缺料壓抑伺服器出貨、新平台放量遞延
至 2027 年，短期獲利持續受到壓抑。研究部因此下修嘉澤 2026、
2027 年稅後 EPS 至 80.59 元（+15.3% YoY，原估 92.36 元）及 
110.19 元（+36.7% YoY，原估 119.88 元）。儘管現價對應 2027 
年本益比約 18 倍，惟考量新平台放量及毛利率修復需待 1H27，
短期營運動能明顯轉弱，故將投資評等由買進調降至中立，目標價 
2,000 元（2027 年 PER 18 倍）。

重點摘要： 
1. 高價原物料成本全面反映，2Q26 獲利大幅低於預期 
嘉澤 2Q26 營收 95.69 億元（+2.6% QoQ、+14.8% YoY），低於原
估 101.35 億元，主因記憶體等零組件缺料壓抑伺服器出貨，消費
性電子需求亦持續疲弱。受到低價原物料庫存於 1Q26 消耗完畢，
金、銅、塑膠等原物料成本全面反映，使原物料占銷貨成本比重由
過往 45-50% 升至 65%，加上新產品仍處學習曲線前期影響，導致
毛利率大幅低於預期，2Q26 毛利率僅 45.47%（-4.08ppt QoQ 、-
5.53ppt YoY） ，低於原估 50.1%。費用亦高於預期，因認列一次性
員工認股費用 2.79 億元，2Q26 營業費用達 22.48 億元（費用率 
23.49%），高於原估的 21.28 億元（原估費用率21.0%）。毛利率
與 費用 率 雙 雙 不 如預 期 下 ， 營益 率 僅 21.97% ，大 幅 低於 原 估 
29.1%。單季稅後純益 19.9 億元（-17.0% QoQ、+165.6% YoY），
EPS 17.68 元，低於原估 21.28 元。

圖一、2024~2027F 嘉澤產品營收比重

資料來源：嘉澤，統一投顧整理

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. 缺料壓抑出貨、毛利率持平谷底，下修 3Q26 獲利預估 
嘉澤 7 月營收 33.69 億元（+8.9% MoM 、+18.6% YoY），對原估 
3Q26 營收達成率僅 30.2%。伺服器 ODM 月產量受記憶體缺料影
響，由 2Q26 的 1.49KK 降至 7、8 月的 1.32KK。毛利率方面，
金、銅、塑膠等原物料成本維持高檔，且為避免市佔流失，未向客
戶轉嫁成本，僅能仰賴新品良率改善與內部降本吸收壓力，毛利率
將持續承壓，預期 3Q26 毛利率僅較 2Q26 小幅回升。研究部下修 
3Q26 營收預估由 111.47 億元至 102.35 億元 （+7.0% QoQ、+21.5% 
YoY），毛利率由原估 50.9% 下修至 45.5%，營益率由原估 29.9% 
下修至 26.5%，稅後 EPS 由原估 23.96 元下修至 19.64 元
（+11.1% QoQ、-4.6% YoY）。

3. 成本維持高檔，2026 年獲利下修 13% 
全年度來看，桌機受 DIY 客戶轉回舊平台影響全年衰退近四成，
筆電受缺料壓抑年減約 14%；伺服器受惠通用型需求強勁與產品組
合升級全年成長約 70%，車用亦維持 15% 成長，惟原物料占銷貨
成本比重升至 65% 的結構下 ， 產品組合優化不足以抵銷成本上升 ，
研究部因此下修嘉澤 2026 年獲利 13%。預估 2026 年合併營收 
400.07 億元（+18.4% YoY，原估 422.42 億元），毛利率 46.7%
（-4.6ppt YoY，原估 50.7%），2026 年稅後 EPS 80.59 元（原
估 92.36 元）。

4. 新平台世代升級與 AI 新應用，成本轉嫁可期 
展望未來 ，Intel Birch Stream 滲透率持續拉升 （2Q26 已占 Intel 
平台約 40%），Oak Stream 與 AMD Venice 新平台將自 4Q26 起
陸續放量 ，SP7 腳位數由 SP5 的 6,096 pins 提升至 7,536 pins，
並同步升級至 PCIe Gen6 ，帶動 Socket ASP 至少提升 15%。此
外，新平台產品依現行原物料價格重新報價，成本得以完全轉嫁，
毛利率可望隨新平台出貨比重提升自 1H27 起明顯回升。AI 新應
用亦逐步展開，QD 液冷快接頭已於 7 月量產並切入一家 CSP 與
一家平台客戶 ，2H26 目標占營收 2-3%；SOCAMM 打入 NVIDIA Vera 
Rubin 且稼動率逾九成；NPO Socket 預計 2027 年量產，中長期
成長動能明確。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

5. 待新平台放量，2027 年獲利重回高成長 
隨新一世代伺服器於 2027 年放量、新平台重新報價與 AI 新產品
貢獻擴大，2027 年獲利可望重回高成長。惟新一世代伺服器放量
時間遞延，出貨貢獻較原估延後，研究部下修 2027 年合併營收至 
496.32 億元 （+24.1% YoY，原估 504.79 億元） 。另外，毛利率則
因重新報價僅適用新平台，加上 QD、線材等低毛利新產品放量將
稀釋毛利率，因此下修毛利率至 49.1%（原估 53.4%）。稅後 EPS 
下修至 110.19 元（原估 119.88 元）。

圖二、伺服器 CPU 產品 Roadmap

資料來源：嘉澤，統一投顧整理

評等  定義

附 錄 二 ： 免 責 宣 言

司 認 可 之 資 料來 源 ， 並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 ， 報
告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊 ， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考 ， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象 提
供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

研究部對桂盟看法依舊正向，主要是考量：(1)歐洲與中國自行車
市場復甦，中低階車款新車製造動能開始回升，桂盟的 OE 訂單恢
復成長；(2)因製造業回流歐洲，公司在歐洲補修 AM 市場或是 OE
市場的市佔率穩定提升 ；(3)非自行車業務持續拓展。汽車正時鏈
條 ATS 與車庫門鏈條 GDO 業務訂單持續增加，公司亦積極佈局鋸
鏈、機器設備等傳動鏈業務，有望進一步擴大營收規模。研究部預
期桂盟後期業績仍能穩健提升 ， 預估2026 年EPS 7.35 元(-4%YOY)、
2027 年EPS 7.80 元(+6%YOY)。以今後 4 個季度 EPS 7.64 元，給
予 15 倍本益比，目標價 115 元，維持買進評等。

重點摘要： 
1. 2Q26財報結果優於預期，本業獲利回升： 
桂盟第2季稅後淨利2.71億元(+20%QOQ，+50%YOY)，2Q26EPS 
2.15元，優於我們之前預估的1.9元，主要是因為季度營收回
升至14.36億元(+7%QOQ，+14%YOY)，高於研究部先前預估的
13.63億元 ， 也讓費用率下降至19.1%， 好於研究部先前預估的
20.0%。儘管因為產品組合差異，使2Q26毛利率較去年同期下
滑 ， 但較前季改善至42.6%(季增0.7個百分點 ， 年減1.5個百分
點) ， 令 桂 盟2Q26 營 業 利 益 回 升 至3.37 億元 (+14%QOQ ，
+22%YOY)，本業獲利回到近七個季度高點，營運改善趨勢確
立。

2. 歐洲中國市場復甦，上半年自行車業務大幅成長 ： 
回顧桂盟2026年上半年主要業務表現：(1)自行車零組件(BC)
占74.9%，成長14%YOY；主要是歐洲與中國自行車市場復甦，
中低階車款新車製造動能開始回升 ，OE訂單恢復成長 ， 且桂盟
在歐洲的 市占率提高， 其AM 營收在該業務的比重提升至
26.3%。(2)機車鏈條(MT)占4.2%， 成長15%YOY。 主要是受惠公
司在印尼市場有加強銷售，有效彌補越南市場的減少。(3)汽
車正時鏈條(ATS)占11.7%， 衰退13%YOY。 主要是因為去年美國
加徵關稅，當時客戶積極備庫存，比較基期較高所致。(4)車
庫門鏈條(GDO)占7.4%，YOY約持平 。 管理層表示 ， 該業務在首
季短暫下降之後 ，2Q26狀況已有回復 。(5)其他業務則占1.8%，
+98%YOY，產品包括：園林工具鋸鏈、機器設備傳動鏈等，因
公司積極拓展其他鏈條商機，且基期低，故有較大增幅。

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

表一 : 桂盟 2Q26 財報結果與預估差異

毛利率 42.6% 42.5% +0.1pct -1.5pct 
費用率 19.1% 20.0% -0.9pct -2.2pct

資料來源：財報、統一投顧整理預估

3. 3Q26營收保持偏強增速： 
由於歐洲與中國自行車行業景氣逐漸回復，桂盟第3季營運有
望維持在相對高檔，研究部預估桂盟3Q26合併營收13.36億元
(-7%QOQ，+21%YOY)。因去年基期較低，本季營收仍有望保持
偏強的同比增速 。 毛利率方面 ， 考量自行車AM占比提高， 加上
ATS的拖累減少 ， 預期本季毛利率有望繼續回升至43.0%。 研究
部預估桂盟3Q26EPS 2.0元 (獲利-7%QOQ，+16%YOY)。

4. 營收優於預期，上修今年獲利7%，預估2026年EPS 7.35元： 
展望2026年 ， 考量桂盟在歐美AM補修市場持續汲取市占 ， 加上
自行車行業景氣回溫 ，OE業務已開始改善 ， 預計全年自行車業
務營收成長16%YOY。 非自行車業務方面 ， 原本擔心的機車MT業
務因印尼銷售增加，預期今年有望持平；而汽車ATS業務與車
庫門GDO業務近期營運皆有改善 ，預期 年度營收分別能有持
平、成長5%YOY的表現。

因1H26營收與費用率表現優於預期， 研究部上修桂盟今年獲
利7%， 預估2026年合併營收53.23億元(+14%YOY)， 年度毛利率
42.3%，費用率20.8%，預估桂盟2026年EPSEPS 7.35元。雖然
今年度獲利預計衰退 4%YOY，但若將1Q25遷廠處份利益扣除
(2025年還原後EPS約為6.3元)，則預期今年調整後獲利將能
成長16%YOY。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

5. 營運穩健增長，預估2027年EPS 7.80元： 
展望2027年 ， 預期歐洲與中國自行車需求將延續回溫趨勢，令
桂盟自行車業務保持3~5%的穩健增速。 其他業務方面 ： 摩托車
MT業務受越南政策轉向電動機車影響而有下滑的風險；但ATS
與GDO業務受惠訂單增加，可望成長8~10%YOY。此外，桂盟積
極拓展園林工具鋸鏈 、 機器設備傳動鏈業務 ， 令其他類營收仍
能有較強的增速 。 整體而言 ， 研究部認為桂盟明年業績將保持
溫和成長態勢，預估桂盟 2027 年EPS有望再回升至 7.80 元
(+6%YOY)。
